PROBLEM
------------------------------
You are an expert at scheduling meetings. You are given a few constraints on the existing schedule of each participant, the meeting duration, and possibly some preferences on the meeting time. Note there exists a solution that works with existing schedule of every participant. Here are a few example tasks and solutions:

TASK: You need to schedule a meeting for Michelle, Steven and Jerry for one hour between the work hours of 9:00 to 17:00 on Monday. 

Here are the existing schedules for everyone during the day: 
Michelle has meetings on Monday during 11:00 to 12:00; 
Steven has blocked their calendar on Monday during 9:00 to 9:30, 11:30 to 12:00, 13:30 to 14:00, 15:30 to 16:00; 
Jerry has blocked their calendar on Monday during 9:00 to 9:30, 10:00 to 11:00, 11:30 to 12:30, 13:00 to 14:30, 15:30 to 16:00, 16:30 to 17:00; 

Find a time that works for everyone's schedule and constraints. 
SOLUTION: 
================================================================================


ENVIRONMENT
------------------------------
You are an expert at scheduling meetings. You are given a few constraints on the existing schedule of each participant, the meeting duration, and possibly some preferences on the meeting time. Note there exists a solution that works with existing schedule of every participant. Here are a few example tasks and solutions:

TASK: You need to schedule a meeting for Michelle, Steven and Jerry for one hour between the work hours of 9:00 to 17:00 on Monday. 

Here are the existing schedules for everyone during the day: 
Michelle has meetings on Monday during 11:00 to 12:00; 
Steven has blocked their calendar on Monday during 9:00 to 9:30, 11:30 to 12:00, 13:30 to 14:00, 15:30 to 16:00; 
Jerry has blocked their calendar on Monday during 9:00 to 9:30, 10:00 to 11:00, 11:30 to 12:30, 13:00 to 14:30, 15:30 to 16:00, 16:30 to 17:00; 

Find a time that works for everyone's schedule and constraints. 
SOLUTION: 
================================================================================


FINAL-PLAN
------------------------------
<domain>
(define (domain meeting_scheduler)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types person slot)
  (:constants michelle steven jerry - person)
  (:predicates
    (next ?s1 ?s2 - slot)
    (available ?p - person ?s - slot)
    (meeting_scheduled)
  )

  ;; The schedule_meeting action looks for a 1-hour block (two consecutive slots).
  ;; It checks that all three participants are available for both 30-minute intervals.
  (:action schedule_meeting
    :parameters (?s1 ?s2 - slot)
    :precondition (and
      (next ?s1 ?s2)
      (available michelle ?s1)
      (available michelle ?s2)
      (available steven ?s1)
      (available steven ?s2)
      (available jerry ?s1)
      (available jerry ?s2)
    )
    :effect (and
      (meeting_scheduled)
    )
  )
)
</domain>
<problem>
(define (problem schedule_monday)
  (:domain meeting_scheduler)
  (:objects
    s1 s2 s3 s4 s5 s6 s7 s8 s9 s10 s11 s12 s13 s14 s15 s16 - slot
  )
  (:init
    ;; Time slot sequence: 30-minute intervals from 09:00 to 17:00
    (next s1 s2)
    (next s2 s3)
    (next s3 s4)
    (next s4 s5)
    (next s5 s6)
    (next s6 s7)
    (next s7 s8)
    (next s8 s9)
    (next s9 s10)
    (next s10 s11)
    (next s11 s12)
    (next s12 s13)
    (next s13 s14)
    (next s14 s15)
    (next s15 s16)

    ;; Michelle's availability: Busy 11:00-12:00 (s5, s6)
    (available michelle s1)
    (available michelle s2)
    (available michelle s3)
    (available michelle s4)
    (available michelle s7)
    (available michelle s8)
    (available michelle s9)
    (available michelle s10)
    (available michelle s11)
    (available michelle s12)
    (available michelle s13)
    (available michelle s14)
    (available michelle s15)
    (available michelle s16)

    ;; Steven's availability: Busy 9:00-9:30 (s1), 11:30-12:00 (s6), 13:30-14:00 (s10), 15:30-16:00 (s14)
    (available steven s2)
    (available steven s3)
    (available steven s4)
    (available steven s5)
    (available steven s7)
    (available steven s8)
    (available steven s9)
    (available steven s11)
    (available steven s12)
    (available steven s13)
    (available steven s15)
    (available steven s16)

    ;; Jerry's availability: Busy 9:00-9:30 (s1), 10:00-11:00 (s3, s4), 11:30-12:30 (s6, s7), 13:00-14:30 (s9, s10, s11), 15:30-16:00 (s14), 16:30-17:00 (s16)
    (available jerry s2)
    (available jerry s5)
    (available jerry s8)
    (available jerry s12)
    (available jerry s13)
    (available jerry s15)
  )
  (:goal
    (meeting_scheduled)
  )
)
</problem> model

DOMAIN
------------------------------
(define (domain meeting_scheduler)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types person slot)
  (:constants michelle steven jerry - person)
  (:predicates
    (next ?s1 ?s2 - slot)
    (available ?p - person ?s - slot)
    (meeting_scheduled)
  )

  ;; The schedule_meeting action looks for a 1-hour block (two consecutive slots).
  ;; It checks that all three participants are available for both 30-minute intervals.
  (:action schedule_meeting
    :parameters (?s1 ?s2 - slot)
    :precondition (and
      (next ?s1 ?s2)
      (available michelle ?s1)
      (available michelle ?s2)
      (available steven ?s1)
      (available steven ?s2)
      (available jerry ?s1)
      (available jerry ?s2)
    )
    :effect (and
      (meeting_scheduled)
    )
  )
)
================================================================================

(define (problem schedule_monday)
  (:domain meeting_scheduler)
  (:objects
    s1 s2 s3 s4 s5 s6 s7 s8 s9 s10 s11 s12 s13 s14 s15 s16 - slot
  )
  (:init
    ;; Time slot sequence: 30-minute intervals from 09:00 to 17:00
    (next s1 s2)
    (next s2 s3)
    (next s3 s4)
    (next s4 s5)
    (next s5 s6)
    (next s6 s7)
    (next s7 s8)
    (next s8 s9)
    (next s9 s10)
    (next s10 s11)
    (next s11 s12)
    (next s12 s13)
    (next s13 s14)
    (next s14 s15)
    (next s15 s16)

    ;; Michelle's availability: Busy 11:00-12:00 (s5, s6)
    (available michelle s1)
    (available michelle s2)
    (available michelle s3)
    (available michelle s4)
    (available michelle s7)
    (available michelle s8)
    (available michelle s9)
    (available michelle s10)
    (available michelle s11)
    (available michelle s12)
    (available michelle s13)
    (available michelle s14)
    (available michelle s15)
    (available michelle s16)

    ;; Steven's availability: Busy 9:00-9:30 (s1), 11:30-12:00 (s6), 13:30-14:00 (s10), 15:30-16:00 (s14)
    (available steven s2)
    (available steven s3)
    (available steven s4)
    (available steven s5)

**Step 1: Schedule the meeting from 14:30 to 15:00 on Monday.**

This 30-minute time slot (identified as timeslot t12 in the model) satisfies all participant constraints:
*   **Roy** is available (he is only blocked earlier in the day).
*   **Kathryn** is available (she is only blocked during 9:30–10:00 and 16:30–17:00).
*   **Amy** is available and her preference is honored, as the meeting concludes at 15:00, which is before her requested threshold of 15:30.

PROBLEM

You are an expert at scheduling meetings. You are given a few constraints on the existing schedule of each participant, the meeting duration, and possibly some preferences on the meeting time. Note there exists a solution that works with existing schedule of every participant. Here are a few example tasks and solutions:

TASK: You need to schedule a meeting for Arthur, Michael and Samantha for one hour between the work hours of 9:00 to 17:00 on Monday. 

Here are the existing schedules for everyone during the day: 
Arthur is busy on Monday during 9:00 to 9:30, 10:30 to 12:00, 16:00 to 17:00; 
Michael has meetings on Monday during 13:00 to 13:30, 14:00 to 14:30; 
Samantha has blocked their calendar on Monday during 10:30 to 11:00, 12:00 to 15:00, 15:30 to 17:00; 

Find a time that works for everyone's schedule and constraints.

You are an expert at scheduling meetings. You are given a few constraints on the existing schedule of each participant, the meeting duration, and possibly some preferences on the meeting time. TASK: You need to schedule a meeting for Thomas, Dylan and Jerry for one hour between the work hours of 9:00 to 17:00 on Monday. 

Here are the existing schedules for everyone during the day: 
Thomas's calendar is wide open the entire day.
Dylan has blocked their calendar on Monday during 10:30 to 11:00, 13:30 to 14:00; 
Jerry is busy on Monday during 9:00 to 11:00, 11:30 to 14:30, 16:00 to 17:00; 

Find a time that works for everyone's schedule and constraints.

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
To schedule the requested meeting based on the provided plan and constraints, follow this instruction:

1.  **Schedule a 1-hour meeting** for Thomas, Dylan, and Jerry from **14:30 to 15:30** on Monday.

This time slot (represented by the consecutive 30-minute blocks **s12** and **s13**) is chosen because:
*   **Thomas** is free all day.
*   **Dylan** is available during this time, as his busy periods end at 11:00 and 14:00.
*   **Jerry** is available during this time, as he is busy from 11:30 to 14:30 and then again from 16:00 to 17:00.
================================================================================


PROBLEM
------------------------------
You are an expert at scheduling meetings. You are given a few constraints on the existing schedule of each participant, the meeting duration, and possibly some preferences on the meeting time. Note there exists a solution that works with existing schedule of every participant. Here are a few example tasks and solutions:

TASK: You need to schedule a meeting for Heather, Nicholas and Zachary for half an hour between the work hours of 9:00 to 17:00 on Monday. 

Here are the existing schedules for everyone during the day: 
Heather has blocked their calendar on Monday during 9:00 to 9:30, 10:30 to 11:00, 13:00 to 14:00, 14:30 to 15:00, 16:00 to 16:30; 
Nicholashas no meetings the whole day.
Zachary has blocked their calendar on Monday during 9:00 to 10:30, 11:00 to 12:00, 12:30 to 13:00, 13:30 to 16:30; 

Zachary would rather not meet on Monday after 14:00. Find a time that works for everyone's schedule and constraints. 
SOLUTION:
